Product Vision & ObjectivesProduct Vision & Objectives – Detailed requirement 1: Explanation of business logic,
validations, edge cases, data handling rules, performance expectations, and user
interaction flow.
Data fields involved: id, reference_id, timestamps, audit flags, tenant_id, created_by,
updated_by, soft_delete flag.
Failure handling: retries, rollback rules, consistency enforcement, offline queue sync.
Compliance impact: consent logs, encryption scope, access traceability.
Example API Flow:
POST /module/action
Request:
{
  "patientId": "UUID",
  "data": "structured object",
  "timestamp": "ISO"
}
Response:
{
  "status": "success",
  "referenceId": "UUID"
}
